  - Answered the alias-storage decision: JSON string with string keys, read back as int keys; legacy arrays unchanged; reading a legacy file with pickling disabled succeeds for all three arrays and fails only on the class_aliases key, which must then be treated as absent
- **[RULE-004](#rule-004-pascal-voc-export-uses-alias-names-and-exclusive-max-bounds)** (P0, Medium) Pascal VOC export uses alias names and exclusive max bounds: OPEN QUESTION, ANSWERED: Pascal VOC exports do NOT have to interoperate with standard VOC tooling.
  - given: replaced the one-line Given with the full precondition - image_size is (HEIGHT, WIDTH), class_order is the ascending sorted unique class ids, class_labels[i] = alias-or-str(id) of class_order[i], channel is the INDEX into class_order, and the pixels must be one CONNECTED block for the example to yield a single object (pascal_voc.py:23,35,37; segment_manager.py:87-95,276,283,397-399; save_export_manager.py:394-431).
  - when: added that Pascal VOC is not in DEFAULT_EXPORT_FORMATS = {NPZ, YOLO_DETECTION} (exporters/__init__.py:60-63), so this path only runs on explicit selection.
  - then: added the \<filename\> element (pascal_voc.py:26), which the card omitted, and replaced the prose Then with the exact file content, verified by executing the legacy exporter.
  - then: added the byte-identical serialization contract the card never mentioned - two-space ET.indent, single-quoted \<?xml version='1.0' encoding='utf-8'?\>, UTF-8, platform newline (CRLF on Windows), no trailing newline, XML escaping of & \< \> in aliases (pascal_voc.py:57-59; verified by running).
  - and: filled in the previously null 'and' with the empty-export behaviour (no file, no delete - pascal_voc.py:52-53, exporters/__init__.py:189-206, save_export_manager.py:106-109), the _LOAD_CHAIN reload precondition (file_manager.py:128-136), and the verified round-trip result.
  - and: corrected the round trip - class 2 alias 'dog' reloads as class_id 0, not class 2, because SegmentManager.clear() wipes aliases before load (segment_manager.py:20-26; file_navigation_manager.py:281; main_window.py:2141). Pixels round-trip exactly; ids do not.
  - and: documented _build_label_map's two-pass order (alias, then integer, then lowest-free-id registered as a new alias) at file_manager.py:345-379, and that it runs over every parsed label BEFORE boxes are clamped or dropped, so a discarded box still burns a class id and creates an alias (verified).
  - parameters: added pose='Unspecified', truncated='0', difficult='0' (pascal_voc.py:41-43), all serialization constants, and the loader constants (defaults, banker's rounding, clamp, collapse-drop, type 'Loaded').
  - edgeCases: rewrote the interop bullet - the card said only 'boxes appear 1 px larger'; the precise statement is that LazyLabel's max values coincide with the devkit's while its min values are one lower, so the extra pixel is on the left/top edge, and a real devkit file loaded into LazyLabel loses its right-most column and bottom row.
  - edgeCases: added one-object-per-contour vs the instances-empty fallback that merges touching same-class objects and reorders output (pascal_voc.py:35; exporters/__init__.py:114-136; test_bbox_roundtrip.py:256-268).
  - edgeCases: added that boxes are measured after crop and pixel priority but in full-image coordinates (segment_manager.py:297-299; file_manager.py:712-739).
  - edgeCases: added the import skip-vs-drop distinction - missing \<name\>/\<bndbox\> skips (file_manager.py:474-477), empty \<xmin/\> raises ValueError and skips (483-490), missing \<xmax\>/\<ymax\> defaults to 0 then drops at the clamp (397-398), missing \<xmin\>/\<ymin\> extends to the image edge.
  - edgeCases: added the OverflowError hole - 'inf'/'1e400' escapes the except at file_manager.py:489, load_existing_mask (202-204) logs and falls through, discarding the whole XML (verified).
  - edgeCases: added banker's rounding on import (file_manager.py:483-486; round(100.5)==100, round(101.5)==102, verified) - JavaScript's Math.round differs.
  - edgeCases: added clamping to [0,w]x[0,h] (file_manager.py:395-396, verified) and that \<size\>, pose, truncated and difficult are ignored on import.
  - edgeCases: added the empty-alias case - text '' serializes as \<name /\> and reloads as the label '0' (verified), which can merge with a real class 0.
  - edgeCases: extended 'depth is always 3' to RGBA and 16-bit, and noted it is never read back, so it is inert - not a bug to fix under the byte-identical mandate.
  - suspectedDefect framing dropped: the exclusive max and depth 3 are no longer treated as defects to fix; per the approved decisions they are preserved and recorded.
  - Noted the stale comment at tests/unit/core/exporters/test_exporters.py:469 ('no loader - write-only format'), contradicted by LOADABLE_FORMATS at test_exporters.py:114-122 and file_manager.py:456. It is a stale comment, not an injection - do not use it to drop the import half of the contract.
- **[RULE-005](#rule-005-yolo-detection-export-line-format)** (P0, Medium) YOLO Detection export line format: No -- YOLO class indices must NOT be renumbered to a contiguous 0..N-1 range, and a TS port that does so is a data-corruption bug.
  - then: replaced the truncated '0.114583...'/'0.020833...' with the exact emitted strings '3 0.1640625 0.11458333333333333 0.015625 0.020833333333333332', re-derived against OpenCV 4.12.0 (yolo_detection.py:27-33).
  - then/and: 'one line per object' corrected to one line per outer contour (RETR_EXTERNAL) per segment (exporters/__init__.py:122-126, segment_manager.py:303-305); the docstring wording at yolo_detection.py:16 flagged as not-the-behavior.
  - parameters: 'floats written unrounded (full precision)' replaced by the exact Python-repr layout rule (exponential iff decpt \<= -4 or decpt \> 16, '.0' appended to whole numbers, exponent signed and zero-padded to 2 digits) plus a validated TypeScript pyRepr() and the measured 421/8013 divergence of String(v).
  - parameters: added that the arithmetic order (x + bw/2)/w must be preserved verbatim for bit-identical doubles.
  - and: added line ordering -- instances order = segment-list order, then OpenCV contour order (lower island first in the verified case); fallback path orders by ascending class id and fuses touching same-class objects.
  - and/parameters: added line terminator semantics -- '\\n' through Python text mode = CRLF on Windows, LF elsewhere, on every line including the last (yolo_detection.py:40-42, verified byte-for-byte).
  - parameters: added open(path,'w') truncate-and-overwrite and os.makedirs(dirname or '.') parent creation (yolo_detection.py:39-40).
  - parameters: added that image_size is (height, width), height first (exporters/__init__.py:102, save_export_manager.py:397).
  - parameters: added the splitext behaviour for multi-dot and extensionless paths (yolo_detection.py:45-46).
  - given: made explicit that the box is measured on pixels surviving crop AND pixel priority (segment_manager.py:297-299) and that the cited pixels must form one 8-connected blob.
  - edgeCases: added that an empty ctx.instances list -- including when every segment is skipped for a mask/shape mismatch (segment_manager.py:294-295) -- silently selects the merged-channel fallback.
  - edgeCases: added that the single-view UI raises ValueError on a 0x0 pixmap before the h\<=0/w\<=0 guard runs (save_export_manager.py:398-399).
  - edgeCases: added the '1.0' whole-number case and the sub-1e-4 scientific-notation case with concrete values.
  - edgeCases: added a do-not-port warning for FileManager.save_bb_txt (file_manager.py:74-123, alias name at line 105) and the load_bb_txt read-back contract (file_manager.py:432-449).
  - open question answered: class ids stay as the original per-image ids; no 0..N-1 renumbering (yolo_detection.py:28, segment_manager.py:87-95, test_bbox_roundtrip.py:513-523).
- **[RULE-007](#rule-007-label-text-to-class-id-resolution-on-import)** (P0, High) Label text to class ID resolution on import: ANSWERED BY DECISION 6, which the owner approved: keep legacy per-image class ids and aliases.
- **[RULE-011](#rule-011-new-segments-take-the-active-class-otherwise-the-next-free-class-id-highest--1)** (P0, Medium) New segments take the active class, otherwise the next free class id (highest + 1): Answered: yes, this rule guards annotation data integrity, and the approver restored it to P0 on 2026-09-17 - the id assigned here IS the id written to every export.
  - Fixed the contradictory When: the card selected "classes 3 and 5" but its own Given plus the add produces classes {0, 3, 4}; there is no class 5. Now selects the class-3 and class-4 segments.
  - Made the Then concrete end-to-end with real numbers: 4 assigned, next_class_id 5, merge target min(3,4)=3, next_class_id recomputed to 4 (segment_manager.py:39, :56, :77, :85, :826).
  - Stated the exact order of checks in add_segment (key present -\> active_class_id !== null -\> next_class_id) and that the id is chosen before the append and before the recompute (:34-39, :55-56).
  - Added that the active-class guard is an explicit null check, so class 0 is a valid active class, and that a truthiness port is wrong (:36).
  - Added that the default applies only when the class_id key is absent, not when its value is null, and that a null id is skipped by the next-id computation and dropped from export (:34, :819-822, :227-228).
  - Corrected the parameters: the id set excludes null values, and next_class_id is a cached field recomputed at :56, :63, :85, :172, :391, :581, :705 - the erase recomputes being conditional on modified_indices.
  - Added the last_toggled_class_id side effect of add_segment (:42) and of toggle_active_class (:412), which the card omitted entirely.
  - Corrected the plain-English claim about merge: it is triggered by M via keyboard_event_manager.py:236-239 -\> assign_segments_to_class, and it uses min of the selected ids, NOT the active class, contradicting the docstrings at keyboard_event_manager.py:237 and segment_manager.py:66.
  - Deduplicated the two identical "gaps are never reused" edge cases (card lines 18 and 20) into one.
  - Made "deleting all class-4 segments makes the next new class 2" well-formed by naming the starting classes {0, 1, 4}.
  - Corrected the reordering edge case: reassign_class_ids remaps aliases from old id to new index and drops aliases whose id is absent from new_order; it does not "drop classes that had no alias". Also flagged the unreachable str(old_id) fallback at :386 and that active_class_id is not remapped.
  - Replaced the vague "each multi-view viewer computes its own next ID" with the actual mechanics: one manager per viewer, the fallback duplicated in the caller and set explicitly (ai_segment_manager.py:366-371; main_window.py:6879-6885), linked polygon finalize sharing one id (main_window.py:5619-5650, 6990-7006), and multi-view bbox/circle falling through to the per-viewer default (main_window.py:5815, 5909).
  - Added that multi-view class clicks use set_active_class, so the click-again-to-deactivate toggle is single-view only (main_window.py:6446, 6456 vs :2699).
  - Added the alias-leak edge case with a worked sequence, and the fact that aliases never reserve ids.
  - Added that every load path passes an explicit class_id (file_manager.py:259, 273, 324, 403, 590, 672, 701) and that annotation import uses a different allocator (file_manager.py:370-377) that must stay separate.
  - Added the undo/redo and AI-to-polygon-conversion paths as cases where the default never fires and the cache stays correct (undo_redo_manager.py:155, :367, :373; segment_manager.py:882, :900).
  - Added the export chain to the And clause so the P0 justification is testable from the card alone (segment_manager.py:87-95, :221-223; save_export_manager.py:400-403, :494-495; exporters/npz.py:23-25).
  - Extended the source list beyond the card's four ranges to the call sites that actually reach the rule: main_window.py:1818-1829, 2000-2005, 5619-5650, 5815, 5909, 6879-6885, 6990-7006; ai_segment_manager.py:283-284, 339, 366-371; keyboard_event_manager.py:236-239; segment_table_manager.py:53-57; polygon_drawing_manager.py:201-206; single_view_mouse_handler.py:426-432, 517-525; single_view_mode.py:157-163, 279-285; file_navigation_manager.py:281.
- **[RULE-014](#rule-014-saved-class-channel-order-is-ascending-class-id-not-the-class-order-table)** (P0, High) Saved class channel order is ascending class ID, not the Class Order table: TWO QUESTIONS, TWO ANSWERS, and the second is decision 6.
- **[RULE-016](#rule-016-propagation-confidence-score-per-object)** (P0, High) Propagation confidence score per object: P0, OVERRIDING THE COMPLIANCE JUDGE.
  - Recorded that the card mixes a data-integrity clause (mask = logit \> 0) with a triage clause (the confidence value), and that the panel split because the two were read as one.
  - Noted the empty-mask case: mean over an empty selection is NaN, and the specified score of 0 is a deliberate guard that a port must reproduce rather than inherit.
- **[RULE-017](#rule-017-sam-2-frame-staging-numbering-gap)** (P0, Medium) SAM 2 frame staging numbering gap: ANSWERED BY READING THE INSTALLED SAM 2, AND THE DEFECT IS REAL.
  - The card's suspectedDefect said masks 'may be' attributed to the wrong image. They WILL be, for every frame after a gap, and the offset is exactly the number of skipped frames before it. Confirmed against the installed sam2 source rather than inferred.
  - Added the mechanism: sam2/utils/misc.py sorts by int(filename) and then addresses frames by list position, so the name is load-bearing for ordering and discarded for identity.
- **[RULE-018](#rule-018-crop-is-clamped-to-the-image-and-blanks-everything-outside-it-on-save-including-the-last-row-and-column)** (P0, Medium) Crop is clamped to the image and blanks everything outside it on save, including the last row and column: Both open questions were decided on 2026-09-17.
  - given: spelled out that the UI takes two ranges (X "-10:1200", Y "50:700"), parsed as bare integers with no validation and pre-swapped by the widget (border_crop_widget.py:115-135), and scoped the rule to single/sequence view.
  - then: replaced the ambiguous 'column 999 and row 700 are always cleared' with the exact kept region (rows [50,700) x columns [0,999), 650x999 = 649,350 positions per channel), named the tensor shape (800, 1000, numClasses) uint8, and stated explicitly that start coordinates are inclusive and end coordinates exclusive.
  - then/and: separated the example-specific clearing from the invariant - the image's last column (width-1) and last row (height-1) can never survive any crop, including the maximum crop (0,0,999,799).
  - and: filled in the previously null field with the copy-not-mutate semantics (file_manager.py:720), the full-image coordinate frame for all exports, the never-read crop_coords field (exporters/__init__.py:107), the per-object intersection (segment_manager.py:297), and the current-image-only scope.
  - parameters: replaced 'corners are rounded' with truncation toward zero on the drag path (single_view_mouse_handler.py:554-555) and integer parsing on the text path, and warned that JS Math.round diverges from Python's round().
  - parameters: restated the drawn-crop gate exactly - rectWidth \> 5 AND rectHeight \> 5, strictly greater, both axes, on the float rectangle before truncation and clamping - and noted typed coordinates bypass it.
  - parameters: removed the duplicated 'x2/y2 exclusive in the mask' clause and the redundant 'x2 \<= width-1 and y2 \<= height-1 by clamping' restatement.
  - parameters: turned the per-image-size memory from a described behavior into an explicit do-not-port instruction, per the 2026-09-17 decision.
  - edgeCases: de-duplicated the two multi-view entries and the restatement of the last-row/column loss.
  - edgeCases: added the degenerate crop that zeroes the entire mask, including the reachable drag that produces it.
  - edgeCases: added the silent discard of a \<= 5 px drag with crop mode retained and the previous crop kept.
  - edgeCases: added SAM model switch/unload as a second crop-clearing trigger (main_window.py:1261, 1287 -\> 3003).
  - edgeCases: added the stale-label hazard when a crop empties the mask (NPZ rewritten as zeros, other formats not deleted).
  - edgeCases: made the multi-view entry concrete - the legacy code raises AttributeError on undefined methods, so multi-view simply has no crop.
  - edgeCases: qualified 'crop does not affect the AI embedding key' - true directly, but with 'operate on view' the key hashes the displayed pixels, which the crop changes when a threshold/FFT/rescale is active.
  - edgeCases: made the sequence 'Save All' entry precise (one active crop applied to every propagated frame, mask size from the on-screen pixmap) and added an explicit do-not-port note for the dead save_npz/save_bb_txt/SaveWorker copies of the crop logic.
- **[RULE-019](#rule-019-merge-selected-segments-into-the-lowest-selected-class)** (P1, Medium) Merge selected segments into the lowest selected class: NO - Merge must NOT use the active class.
  - Answered the open SME question: Merge does not read the active class, and the docstrings that say it does are wrong.
  - Recorded why, in terms of which state is visible to the user at the moment they press M, rather than as a preference between two plausible behaviours.
  - Confirmed the panel's not-P0 rating with a reason: no data is destroyed, masks are untouched, and the change is undoable and explicitly reversible via RULE-013.
- **[RULE-023](#rule-023-propagation-seeds-come-only-from-mask-segments-on-reference-frames)** (P1, Medium) Propagation seeds come only from mask segments on reference frames: YES - rasterize drawn polygons, boxes and circles and use them as propagation seeds.
  - Answered the open SME question: yes, drawn shapes are rasterized and seeded, using the same rasterizer the exporter uses so the seed matches what would be saved.
  - Named the reporting obligation the card did not carry: a segment that rasterizes to nothing is skipped and SAID, rather than silently dropped as legacy does with every shape.
  - Recorded that object ids keep reference-frame order with rasterized shapes in place, not appended, so ids do not depend on representation.
  - Confirmed not-P0 with a reason - nothing is destroyed - while noting the failure is invisible, which is why it stays P1.
- **[RULE-040](#rule-040-yolo-detection-import-validation-rounding-and-clamping)** (P0, Medium) YOLO Detection import validation, rounding and clamping: The open question is whether this card should keep its P0 'copy Python's half-to-even rounding exactly' contract, and the approved decisions settle it: yes.
  - given: replaced the ambiguous '640x480 image' with the actual API contract image_size = (height, width) = (480, 640) (file_manager.py:391, :421), and added the preconditions — empty segment list and empty class_aliases via _reset_state/SegmentManager.clear (main_window.py:2141, segment_manager.py:19-26), and .txt being the last chain entry (:128-136).
  - then: kept the worked example but made it verifiable — showed every round() input, the exact 38,400-pixel extent, the literal segment record {type:'Loaded', vertices:null, class_id:3}, and the IEEE-754 detail that 1.1-0.1 is exactly 1.0 while 1.1+0.1 is 1.2000000000000002, so line 3's raw x2 is 768 and the drop comes from x alone.
  - and: filled the previously null field with the class-id-before-clamp ordering (:392 before :394-398), the leftover class_aliases[0]='cat' (:377) and its propagation into saves, plus the all-or-nothing nature of the parse loop (:453).
  - parameters: removed the duplicated rounding clause; added image_size ordering, the exact denormalize association with a do-not-refactor warning, a reference half-to-even pyRound implementation with ten verified ties, the asymmetric clamp, the explicit drop predicate, the Python float() and int() grammars versus JavaScript's, Python's whitespace split semantics, and the full two-pass class-id resolution algorithm (:345-379).
  - edgeCases: added the whole-file abort on 'nan'/'inf'/'1e309'/overflow (:437-440 vs :446-449 vs :453, caught at :202-204) as the leading case — the card omitted it entirely.
  - edgeCases: added the distinction between that abort and the clean OSError/UnicodeDecodeError return at :426-428.
  - edgeCases: added the platform-dependent encoding of open() at :424 and the UTF-8 BOM corrupting the first class token into a new named class.
  - edgeCases: added that the class token is never validated, that an alias name beats the integer interpretation (:362-366), and the behaviour of '3.0', '-1' and oversized integer labels.
  - edgeCases: generalised the card's 'cat' note into the ordering rule that ids and aliases are assigned for all parsed boxes before clamping (:392 before :394-398).
  - edgeCases: replaced 'negative coordinates are clamped to 0' with the full drop rule covering flipped boxes and boxes entirely outside the image (:395-398).
  - edgeCases: added two concrete rounding-tie cases, including the 1024-wide one where half-up keeps a box that legacy drops — proving the tie is not merely cosmetic.
  - edgeCases: corrected 'a caption .txt still stops the load chain' — .txt is already last (:135); the real effect is find_annotation_file marking the image as labelled (:138-151, main_window.py:3966/:4226/:4858).
  - edgeCases: added duplicate/overlapping boxes producing separate segments, blank lines being 0 tokens, and the per-segment full (h,w) mask allocation cost (:400).
  - edgeCases: added the destructive downstream consequence — auto-save on navigate plus delete-all-sidecars-when-empty (file_navigation_manager.py:156-160, save_export_manager.py:106-109, exporters/__init__.py:208-215) — which is why the zero-segment cases matter.
- **[RULE-041](#rule-041-yolo-segmentation-import-validation)** (P0, Medium) YOLO Segmentation import validation: Open question, answered from the approved decisions: KEEP the legacy whole-file stop for a non-finite or overflowing coordinate -- do not change it to per-line skipping -- but stop presenting the result as an empty or silently-degraded document.
  - given: spelled out that image_size is (h, w) = (480, 640) height-first and that SegmentManager starts empty (aliases {}, next_class_id 0), which the class-id assertions depend on (file_manager.py:551; file_navigation_manager.py:281).
  - when: named the load-chain position and the try/except Exception wrapper that turns a parse error into a silent format downgrade (file_manager.py:128-136, :187-205).
  - then: kept the vertices [[64,48],[128,48],[128,96]] and class 0, but replaced 'mask = filled triangle' with the exact raster -- 1617 pixels, rows 48..96, row 48 = x 64..128, row 96 = x 128 only, boundary included, and (65,49) set although geometrically outside (file_manager.py:583-585).
  - then: stated that parts[0] is the label and is never numerically parsed (file_manager.py:568, :577).
  - and (new): added the full cv2.fillPoly algorithm -- 8-connected Bresenham outline union even-odd 16.16 fixed-point scanline fill, with the exact slope truncation and ceil/floor span rounding (file_manager.py:584).
  - and (new): added that class ids and aliases are assigned before the empty-mask drop (file_manager.py:579 vs :587-588).
  - and (new): added the three distinct failure modes -- whole-file abort on non-finite coordinates (:573 outside the try at :567-570), partial load on int32 overflow (:584), and silent zero-segment return with NO fallback on an undecodable file (:556-558, :205).
  - parameters: replaced 'rounding Python round()' with round-half-to-even on the IEEE-754 double product plus two worked ties (0.16015625*640 = 102.5 -\> 102; 0.171875*480 = 82.5 -\> 82) and the JS Math.round divergence (file_manager.py:573).
  - parameters: added that x scales by w and y by h from an (h, w) tuple, with no clamping (file_manager.py:551, :573).
  - parameters: added the exact float() grammar accepted and rejected, and that split() handles tabs, runs of spaces and CRLF (file_manager.py:562, :568).
  - parameters: de-duplicated 'min tokens 7' / 'Minimum tokens 7' and recorded that the len(points) \>= 3 check at :576 is dead code.
  - edgeCases: merged the two duplicate 'Loaded segments cannot be vertex-edited' entries into one, with the real gate cited (edit_mode_manager.py:108-112; main_window.py:2484; segment_manager.py:853-857).
  - edgeCases: corrected 'a polygon fully outside the image yields no pixels and is dropped' to note that zero-area polygons touching the image are KEPT (1 px / 129 px) and only fully off-image shapes vanish (file_manager.py:587-588).
  - edgeCases: added the nan/inf whole-file abort, the int32 partial load, the any-string label, the numeric-before-named id assignment, the alias-named-'0' precedence, the id burned by a dropped polygon, the unclamped vertices with a clipped mask, the x == 1.0 -\> column w drop, even-odd pentagram fill, the locale-encoding read, and the blank-line skip.
  - edgeCases: kept the 5-token '_seg.txt' case and strengthened it -- it also blocks the fallback chain, because no exception is raised (file_manager.py:205).
  - source: the rule spans more than file_manager.py:542-600; it also depends on :128-136 (load chain), :187-205 (fallback), :345-379 (label map) and core/segment_manager.py:28-56 (add_segment).
- **[RULE-042](#rule-042-class-alias-editing-is-unvalidated)** (P1, Medium) Class alias editing is unvalidated: ALIASES MUST BE NON-EMPTY AND TRIMMED; DUPLICATES ARE ALLOWED BUT WARNED; AND A DISPLAY MARKER MUST NEVER REACH A FILE.
- **[RULE-045](#rule-045-crop-coordinate-validation-and-reuse-by-image-size)** (P1, Medium) Crop coordinate validation and reuse by image size: ANSWERED BY DECISION 9, which the owner approved: per-image-size crop restore does NOT carry over.
- **[RULE-051](#rule-051-supported-image-formats)** (P2, Medium) Supported image formats: ANSWERED BY DECISION 9, and the referee's citation correction is adopted.
- **[RULE-054](#rule-054-closing-the-application-never-saves-the-open-images-annotations)** (P0, Medium) Closing the application never saves the open image's annotations: ANSWERED BY DECISION 7, which the owner approved: do not reproduce the silent loss on close.
- **[RULE-057](#rule-057-multi-view-batch-navigation-always-saves-ignoring-the-auto-save-setting)** (P0, High) Multi-view batch navigation always saves, ignoring the Auto-Save setting: ANSWERED BY DECISIONS 7 AND 8 TOGETHER, and the second is what makes it simple.
- **[RULE-059](#rule-059-auto-save-current-image-before-switching-images-single-view)** (P0, Medium) Auto-save current image before switching images (single view): Decided 2026-09-17, before Phase 2 scaffolds anything, because this rule gates that phase.
  - Recorded the decision that auto-save on navigation is kept, with dirty tracking and a successful-load token, rather than replaced by explicit-save-only.
- **[RULE-065](#rule-065-selecting-a-file-in-the-sequence-tab-jumps-to-its-frame-a-file-outside-the-timeline-bounces-back)** (P1, Medium) Selecting a file in the Sequence tab jumps to its frame; a file outside the timeline bounces back: TWO QUESTIONS, BOTH ANSWERED BY DECISION 7 AND BY WHAT THE COMMENT ALREADY INTENDED.
- **[RULE-067](#rule-067-crop-persistence-across-image-navigation)** (P1, Medium) Crop persistence across image navigation: ANSWERED BY DECISION 9: a crop applies to the current image only.
- **[RULE-078](#rule-078-annotations-load-from-the-best-file-present-and-a-damaged-non-npz-file-stops-the-search)** (P0, Medium) Annotations load from the best file present, and a damaged non-NPZ file stops the search: ANSWER TO THE OPEN QUESTION -- split the legacy behavior in two.
  - Narrowed the rule's central claim: replaced 'a damaged non-NPZ file stops the search' with 'the chain continues only when a loader raises' -- a structurally corrupt _CM.npz also falls through (file_manager.py:309 catches only (OSError, ValueError); numpy 2.2.6 raises EOFError / pickle.UnpicklingError / zipfile.BadZipFile, verified empirically).
  - Replaced the edge case 'Only a corrupt .npz (np.load raises) falls through to the next format' with the two-loader version, and listed the five loaders that genuinely swallow-and-stop with their exact catch tuples and line numbers.
  - Restated the selection predicate as 'the first existing file whose loader returns without raising' (:173-176 / :202-204 / :205), replacing both 'the first format present' and any 'first that loads something' reading.
  - Added the stop-with-zero-segments-and-no-error cases the card omitted: .npz with no mask/masks key (:241-242), all-empty mask channels, CreateML non-list root (:509-510), COCO non-object root (:621-623).
  - Merged the card's three duplicate image-size edge cases into one and corrected the mechanics: QImageReader header read with a full cv2.imread fallback (:207-222), resolved lazily on the first non-NPZ hit and cached for the rest of the loop (:178-179), and aborting the WHOLE chain rather than one format (:180-185); named the callers that reach it (main_window.py:3619, :4864, :6112-6114).
  - Filled the previously null 'and' field with the fully cited destructive tail: settings.py:45 -\> file_navigation_manager.py:156-160 -\> save_export_manager.py:106-109 -\> :523-529 -\> exporters/__init__.py:209-215 -\> yolo_detection.py:48-53, plus the 'Deleted:' toast at :536-538.
  - Made the 'then' a complete worked trace with real numbers, including the two boxes cat.txt would have produced -- class 0 at (256, 216, 384, 264) and class 1 at (128, 312, 192, 408) at 640x480, per the denormalization at :446-449.
  - Named the concrete trigger for the mixed-state edge case: a COCO Infinity coordinate raising OverflowError at :659/:687, uncaught by the (TypeError, ValueError) handlers at :662/:690, versus NaN which raises ValueError and is caught; added _restore_aliases at :232 as the NPZ equivalent.
  - Added the find_annotation_file aggravator (:139-151): existence-only, so it disagrees with the chain's actual result and still marks a zero-segment image as annotated, driving propagation skips at main_window.py:3966 / :4218-4227.
  - Gave the base-name-collision edge case a worked consequence (foo_CM.png loads foo.png's class map, gets 0 segments, then auto-save deletes foo.png's class map) instead of stating the collision abstractly.
  - Corrected the provenance in the defect claim: CHANGELOG.md:12 (2.0.8) fixed the missing-image_size cause of this same data-loss sequence but not the swallow-and-stop cause; CHANGELOG.md:24, ARCHITECTURE.md:204-207 and the docstring at file_manager.py:160-163 all state the fall-through intent, and ARCHITECTURE.md:193 asserts a user-cleared precondition for delete_all_outputs that nothing enforces.
  - Added the test-coverage edge case: only the NPZ leg is pinned (test_bbox_roundtrip.py:741-756); the LOAD_PRIORITY/_LOAD_CHAIN sync rests on test_bbox_roundtrip.py:494-500.
  - Added a security note on np.load(allow_pickle=True) at :231 and :297 so the rewrite does not reproduce a code-executing deserializer.
  - Answered the open question explicitly: the priority order is the contract to reproduce; the silent empty load and the delete-on-empty that follows are the defect to fix.
- **[RULE-081](#rule-081-propagation-never-overwrites-reference-frames-and-by-default-frames-already-labeled)** (P0, Medium) Propagation never overwrites reference frames and, by default, frames already labeled: The open question is whether a bare sibling \<stem\>.txt (or any other same-stem file that is not really an annotation) should count as 'already labeled'.
  - Rewrote Given to pin the real preconditions: UI-started run, Keep Flagged off, threshold 0.99, default export formats NPZ + YOLO Detection, auto-save on, and frame 30 labeled via _coco.json rather than an unspecified format.
  - Rewrote When to state that the labeled set is a one-time snapshot taken before the worker starts, and that SAM 2 still processes every frame (the flag is UI-side only).
  - Made Then a full worked example with concrete indices, confidences (0.997/0.995), file names, RGB values and the exact notification strings ('Saving 2 frames...', 'Saved 1 frames to NPZ').
  - Added the precondition to 'frame 31 gets masks': every object must clear 0.99, else with Keep Flagged off all masks for the frame are discarded and it is flagged red.
  - Qualified the brown paint: it only occurs if the engine yields at least one non-empty result; all-empty frames stay gray/pending.
  - Added the And clause documenting the two-store double gate, the over-counted progress/remaining messages, and the dormant PropagationSaveWorker that would bypass the rule.
  - Corrected Parameters: recorded BOTH defaults (UI true, engine false at main_window.py:4017/4181/4200), noted the setting is not persisted, and added threshold, keep-flagged, export-format and auto-save defaults plus the exact status colors.
  - Kept the seven-suffix list but made it explicit that the check is existence-only, same directory, same stem, in priority order, with no parsing.
  - Replaced the vague 'propagated masks overwrite those files' edge case with the precise write semantics (enabled formats only; .npz shadows _coco.json; .txt genuinely overwritten).
  - Added the delete_all_outputs edge case — the only path that deletes user annotation files, reachable from auto-save when a frame ends up with zero segments.
  - Added the ordering edge case: the skip check precedes the buffer commit, so skipped/reference frames do not flush the prior frame's buffer.
  - Added the SKIPPED-status exception and the brown-color overloading with dimension-mismatch frames.
  - Added that propagation writes nothing to disk, so a post-propagation 'file untouched' assertion is vacuous — the contract test must assert at Save All and at auto-save.
  - Added that reference frames are excluded from the skip set and protected by four independent guards, so turning Skip Labeled off does not expose them.
  - Noted Save All is irreversible (masks dropped on mark_frame_saved, undo history cleared on navigation).
- **[RULE-082](#rule-082-save-all-propagated-frames-eligibility)** (P0, Medium) Save All propagated frames eligibility: P0, OVERRIDING THE COMPLIANCE JUDGE, and both product questions answered.
- **[RULE-083](#rule-083-saving-an-image-with-no-segments-deletes-all-of-its-annotation-files)** (P0, Medium) Saving an image with no segments deletes all of its annotation files: DECISION (already made by the product owner, recorded here so no implementer re-opens it): the rewrite uses explicit save with dirty tracking and NEVER deletes an annotation file without an explicit user action, so this legacy behaviour is a defect to be fixed, not reproduced - deletion must not be inferred from an empty in-memory segment list, must not extend to formats the user did not select, and must not touch files LazyLabel did not write in the current session.
  - Given: added the missing preconditions - single-view mode, Auto-Save on Navigate ON (default), no pending polygon points, no pending AI preview - and stated explicitly that the card documents legacy behaviour, not target behaviour.
  - When: expanded from 2 triggers to the 5 that reach save_single_view_output (Enter, file-tree selection, next/previous navigation, sequence frame change, Save All Propagated) with the auto_save gate identified per trigger, plus the separate multi-view family that bypasses auto_save entirely.
  - Then: fixed the deletion/notification ORDER to registration order - the notice is exactly "Deleted: cat_coco.json, cat.npz, cat.txt", not the card's implied order; added that all seven candidate paths are probed and the four absent ones skipped, that no confirmation and no undo exist, and kept the 2-segment contrast case.
  - And: filled in the previously null field with the no-provenance/no-dirty-flag clause, the multi-view divergence (ignores auto_save, silent, false success message, only viewers 0 and 1), and the non-transactional partial-deletion behaviour with its differing error handling per path.
  - Parameters: rewrote the garbled triple-concatenation into one statement; kept the seven suffixes (re-verified char-for-char) but put them in real iteration order with per-file citations, added the exact user-visible strings, the auto_save default, and the fact that the selected-format set is read only after the early return.
  - Edge cases: deduplicated (the 'No image loaded' case appeared twice, the unrelated-.txt/.xml case three times) and reduced the crop case to its true scope (write path only, no effect on deletion).
  - Edge cases: CORRECTED the failed-load case - a raising loader is caught and the chain continues (file_manager.py:202-204); only a silently-empty loader stops the chain at 205 and dooms the healthy lower-priority files.
  - Edge cases: added multi-view deletes with Auto-Save OFF; multi-view deletes silently while Enter reports success; multi-view only touches viewers 0 and 1 (main_window.py:6581).
  - Edge cases: added Save All Propagated as an ungated trigger that deletes a frame's files and still marks the frame saved (main_window.py:4781-4784, 4813-4818).
  - Edge cases: added Enter in sequence view + polygon mode does nothing at all (keyboard_event_manager.py:193-230 has no sequence branch).
  - Edge cases: added the undecodable-image paths - load_image_by_path clears segments before cv2.imread fails (file_navigation_manager.py:276-302), and load_selected_image returns before _reset_state so the previous image's segments end up attached to the new path (162 vs 175-176 vs 186).
  - Edge cases: added non-transactional partial deletion and its divergent error handling (save_export_manager.py:106-111 vs main_window.py:3498-3517 vs 6588/6635).
  - Edge cases: added the write-path asymmetry - a SELECTED format can be skipped when it produces no annotations, leaving a stale file of that format (npz.py:15-17, yolo_detection.py:19-22/35-36, coco.py:85, createml.py:51, npz_class_map.py:22-26, yolo_segmentation.py:28/46).
  - Edge cases: REFUTED the judge's 'eighth file' finding - \<base\>.json is deleted only from dead code (_delete_multi_view_files at save_export_manager.py:544-567, reachable only from _save_viewer_output at 454-521, which has no callers); recorded as must-not-reproduce.
  - Edge cases: added the doc/code contradictions as an explicit do-not-trust note (core/exporters/__init__.py:192-196, ARCHITECTURE.md:193, main_window.py:3499 and 3515).
  - Answer: recorded the settled decision that the rewrite never deletes without explicit user action, so this rule is captured as a legacy defect for migration tests rather than as target behaviour.
- **[RULE-084](#rule-084-ai-features-require-segment-anything-and-pytorch-271)** (P1, Medium) AI features require segment-anything and PyTorch 2.7.1+: ANSWERED, AND ALREADY BUILT IN PHASE 2.
  - Confirmed rather than assumed the ValueError path: the card's edge case is accurate and the consequence is that a newer-than-required PyTorch prevents startup entirely.
  - Recorded that the availability answer now travels over /health with its reason, rather than being a local boolean, because three processes need it.
- **[RULE-085](#rule-085-model-type-detected-from-file-name)** (P1, Medium) Model type detected from file name: EXPLICITLY, FROM A MANIFEST - not from the file name, and not from checkpoint metadata either.
  - Rejected checkpoint metadata as the source as well as the file name: reading it requires unpickling an untrusted file (SEC-01), and a third-party fine-tune may not carry it.
  - Recorded that the worst case is the one that does NOT error - sam2_hiera_large_tuned.pt builds a tiny config around large weights and quietly degrades output.
  - Recorded the consequence of the manifest: a checkpoint not listed is not loadable, replacing legacy's recursive search-and-guess.
- **[RULE-086](#rule-086-active-class-toggle-and-recent-class-hotkey)** (P1, Medium) Active class toggle and recent-class hotkey: THE LIVE CODE WINS, and the referee's citation correction is adopted.
- **[RULE-087](#rule-087-default-model-download-integrity-check)** (P1, Medium) Default model download integrity check: YES TO BOTH, AND THE RUNTIME DOWNLOAD GOES AWAY ENTIRELY.
  - Recorded that legacy's Content-Length check does not catch its own target case, because the partial file survives the network error and the next start skips the download rather than retrying it.
  - Strengthened the answer beyond 'discard partial downloads' to 'do not download at runtime', which makes the partial-file case unreachable rather than handled.
  - Noted that hash verification does not replace weights-only loading; Phase 3 exit criterion 3 needs both.
- **[RULE-091](#rule-091-up-to-10-image-embeddings-are-cached-by-file-path-and-computed-ahead-for-nearby-images)** (P1, Medium) Up to 10 image embeddings are cached by file path and computed ahead for nearby images: BOTH QUESTIONS ANSWERED, AND THE SECOND ONE IS WORSE THAN THE CARD SUGGESTS.
  - The card's suspectedDefect said stale embeddings 'may be' reused after switching models. Verified that they WILL be, and without error: every SAM 1 variant emits a 256 x 64 x 64 embedding, so the shapes match and nothing checks anything else.
  - Added that path-keyed caching is also wrong under Operate On View, where the encoded pixels change while the path does not - a case the card did not consider.
  - Named the three-part cache key that makes invalidation structural rather than a step someone must remember.
- **[RULE-092](#rule-092-linked-multi-view-shape-mirroring-and-class-ids)** (P1, Medium) Linked multi-view shape mirroring and class ids: YES - in linked mode one linked operation makes ONE object, so it must carry the same class in both images.
  - Answered the open SME question: yes, a linked operation gives the same class in both images, and the per-tool inconsistency is a defect to delete rather than port.
  - Corrected what 'the same class id' can mean under decision 6: ids are per image, so copying the number produces matching values that may name different classes. The invariant is the alias; the id is resolved per image from it.
  - Named the one case where the numeric id must match after all - a class with no alias, where legacy writes str(class_id) and the id IS the name.
  - Recorded the genuinely open part as unresolved rather than deciding it here: reconciling two images that already disagree is the per-viewer class-id question decision 8 schedules for Phase 6 entry.

## Candidate rules rejected by the citation referees

Kept for audit. A rejected rule is not behavior to preserve.

| Name | Cited source | Referee verdict |
|---|---|---|
| Operate On View: which image SAM segments | `legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/main_window.py:1576-1622; legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:235-251; legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/managers/image_adjustment_manager.py:604-643; legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/main_window.py:2875-2904` | refuted: Refuted. The synchronous half of the rule is right. The lazy half describes code that never runs. What holds: - E:/GitHub/LazyLabel/legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/main_window.py:1576-1603. When Operate On View (OOV) is on, SAM gets `active_viewer._adjusted_pixmap`, converted from BGRA to RGB. That pixmap has saturation, brightness/contrast and gamma applied (E:/GitHub/LazyLabel/legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/photo_viewer.py:95-171). The cache key is MD5 of the pixels plus the threshold and FFT params (main_window.py:2875-2904). - main_window.py:1604-1622. When OOV is off, the key is MD5(path) and SAM loads the file with `set_image_from_path`. - The cache holds 10 entries (main_window.py:223, `EmbeddingCacheManager(max_size=10)`) with LRU eviction (E:/GitHub/LazyLabel/legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/managers/embedding_cache_manager.py:70-71). - This path is called from the image-load code: `load_selected_image` and `load_image_by_path` (E:/GitHub/LazyLabel/legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/managers/file_navigation_manager.py:201, 222, 366). What fails (the lazy path): - The OOV branch at E:/GitHub/LazyLabel/legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:235-242 only runs if `self.mw._cached_original_image is not None`. - On MainWindow that attribute is only ever set to None (main_window.py:190 and 2164). Nothing else in legacy/lazylabel, tests included, writes to it. - The real image cache is a separate attribute on ImageAdjustmentManager (E:/GitHub/LazyLabel/legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/managers/image_adjustment_manager.py:49, 480). That class is a component of MainWindow, not a base class (main_window.py:77, 645). - So lines 240-242 never run, and `get_current_modified_image` (image_adjustment_manager.py:604-643) is never reached from this path. What the lazy path actually does: - The cache key is always MD5(path) (sam_single_view_manager.py:245), even with OOV on. `current_image` stays None. - The cache lookup at lines 256-267 uses that path key, so it can restore embeddings of the unmodified image. - `SAMUpdateWorker` receives `current_image=None` and falls back to `set_image_from_path` (E:/GitHub/LazyLabel/legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/workers/sam_update_worker.py:45-56). - Threshold, rescale and FFT slider changes with OOV on only mark SAM dirty (image_adjustment_manager.py:227-228, 268-269, 305-306). `sam_update_timer.start` is never called, so the debounced synchronous path does not run either. - Net effect: after a slider change with OOV on, SAM re-embeds the original file from disk, not the rescale + threshold + FFT output. Corrected rule: - OOV is only honored when `_update_sam_model_image` runs, which happens on image load. - The lazy single-view path ignores OOV and always uses the original file, keyed by MD5(path). - The guard checks the wrong object. That looks like a latent bug, but this is my inference; no comment confirms intent. Question for an SME: when OOV is on, should SAM keep using the modified image after slider changes? I found no instruction-like text and no credentials in the cited regions. |
| Loading a different model resets prompts, crop and undo but keeps segments; Unload is undone by the next AI click | `legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/main_window.py:1214-1305; legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/main_window.py:2956-3037; legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:117-202; legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:218-229; legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/managers/sam_multi_view_manager.py:92-181; legacy/lazylabel/src/lazylabel/ui/managers/sam_multi_view_manager.py:204-206` | refuted: All paths are under E:\\GitHub\\LazyLabel\\legacy\\lazylabel\\src\\lazylabel\\. Much of the reset logic described is real code. However, the spec's trigger can't happen in the running app, and one core claim is contradicted by the code, so the lines do not implement the rule as written. WHAT HOLDS (real code): - **AI extras check:** without the AI extras, Load shows the install hint (ui/main_window.py:1236-1238). - **Reset when a model is already loaded:** _load_model calls _reset_sam_state_for_model_switch only if a model is available (ui/main_window.py:1260-1261). - It stops the image-embedding worker (ui/main_window.py:2960-2972). This works because the main window's worker field forwards to the single-view manager (ui/main_window.py:272-279, ui/managers/sam_worker_manager.py:79-87). - It clears points (2985), the preview (2990-2993), the crop visuals and state (2996-3003) and undo history (3025-3026). It then redraws the segments (3037). - **Background load and label:** the model loads on a background thread (ui/main_window.py:1272-1274, ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:117-151). The label reads "Loading: \<file name\>" (ui/main_window.py:1276-1279). - **Success:** shows "AI model ready for prompting" and requests a new embedding of the current image (ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:173-184). - **Failure:** the status bar shows "AI model failed: ..." and the buttons switch to the not-loaded state (ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:192-197). - **Unload** frees the model (ui/main_window.py:1287-1305). - **Single view:** the next AI click (ai_segment_manager.py:431, modes/single_view_mode.py:29, main_window.py:2219) clears the unloaded flag and starts a load (ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:218-229). - **Multi-view:** after an explicit unload, automatic loading is blocked (ui/managers/sam_multi_view_manager.py:204-206). WHAT FAILS: 1. **The spec's click can't happen.** After any successful load, set_model_loaded_state(True) (ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:171) disables the Load button (ui/widgets/model_selection_widget.py:212-215). - Only Unload (ui/main_window.py:1304) or a load error (ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:197) turns it back on. Picking a model in the dropdown does not (ui/widgets/model_selection_widget.py:182-188). - _load_model can only be reached from that button (ui/widgets/model_selection_widget.py:177, ui/control_panel.py:558, ui/main_window.py:875). - So "Default SAM loaded, then the user clicks Load Model" can't happen in normal use. The only exception is a second click during a load, and then start_initialization returns False (ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:123-124), so the chosen model never loads. - In practice a user switches with Unload then Load, and the reset comes from Unload (ui/main_window.py:1287). 2. **"Does nothing if the same custom model is loaded" is wrong.** The check reads sam_model.custom_model_path (ui/main_window.py:1247-1249). Neither model class has that attribute; both store current_model_path instead (models/sam_model.py:87, models/sam2_model.py:54). As a result: - The check always treats the loaded model as the default one. - Selecting the custom model that is already loaded triggers a full reset and reload. - Selecting Default while a custom model is loaded is refused with "Model already loaded". 3. **"Silently loads it again" is wrong on two counts.** - The pending custom model path is cleared after every successful load (ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:158), so the auto-reload loads the Default model (ui/workers/single_view_sam_init_worker.py:43-48), not the custom model that was unloaded. - It is not silent: it shows "Initializing AI model..." (ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:133) and rejects the click (ui/managers/ai_segment_manager.py:434-436). 4. **Re-embedding only happens by accident.** The embedding cache is keyed only by the image path hash and is never cleared (ui/managers/sam_single_view_manager.py:245,256-267; ui/managers/embedding_cache_manager.py:40-56). - In the spec's SAM1-to-SAM2 case, restoring the cached SAM1 data fails on a missing 'orig_hw' key (models/sam2_model.py:471,476-478), so the image is embedded again. - When switching within the same family (SAM1 to SAM1, or SAM2 to SAM2), the old model's cached embeddings would be reused instead. 5. **The multi-view failure latch outlives a model switch.** The failure flag is only reset in cleanup() (ui/managers/sam_multi_view_manager.py:370), which only the reset method calls (ui/main_window.py:2976). - That reset runs only when a single-view model is loaded (ui/main_window.py:1260, 1283-1285). If none is loaded, the flag can stay set for the whole session. - reset_init_failed() is never called. No text aimed at AI tools and no credentials were found in the cited code. |

## Near-duplicate cards folded into another card

The three extraction lenses often described the same behavior. Each group below was reviewed and merged into one card: sources, edge cases, parameters, suspected defects and SME questions were unioned, and the highest priority and the lowest confidence were kept. The folded card's own outcome is listed here so nothing a lens recorded is lost.

| Folded card | Lens category | Kept as | Folded card's outcome (Then) |
|---|---|---|---|
| COCO JSON export: categories, bbox, area, polygon | Calculation | [RULE-001](#rule-001-coco-json-export-structure-and-area) | categories=[{id:5,name:'dog',supercategory:'animal'}]; annotation {id:1,image_id:1,category_id:5,bbox:[10,10,10,10],area:81,segmentation:[[10,10,10,19,19,19,19,10]],iscrowd:0} And area = contour polygon area when the outline has 3+ points, otherwise bbox width x height; an alias with no dot uses the same text for name and supercategory; no file when there are no objects |
| CreateML export uses pixel center coordinates | Calculation | [RULE-002](#rule-002-createml-exportimport-pixel-center-boxes) | img_createml.json annotation {label 'dog', coordinates {x 105.0, y 55.0, width 10, height 10}} |
| NPZ Class Map export/import with foreground mask | Calculation | [RULE-003](#rule-003-npz-class-map-export-resolves-overlaps-to-lowest-class-and-stores-foreground) | class_map is 2 at the overlap and 0 at the class-0 pixel with foreground True; reload creates one segment per class id found in the foreground And No file when no pixel is labeled; on load a class map whose shape differs from the image is rejected; files without 'foreground' treat 0 as background |
| NPZ Class Map import requires matching size | Validation | [RULE-003](#rule-003-npz-class-map-export-resolves-overlaps-to-lowest-class-and-stores-foreground) | Segments for class 0 (the foreground zeros) and class 4 |
| Pascal VOC export/import: 0-based exclusive max bounds, depth always 3 | Calculation | [RULE-004](#rule-004-pascal-voc-export-uses-alias-names-and-exclusive-max-bounds) | \<base\>.xml has name 'cat', pose 'Unspecified', truncated 0, difficult 0, xmin 100, ymin 50, xmax 300, ymax 150, size depth 3; reload fills exactly columns 100-299, rows 50-149 And Missing bndbox fields default to '0'; missing name defaults to '0'; objects without name or bndbox are skipped |
| YOLO Detection export: normalized center/size box per object | Calculation | [RULE-005](#rule-005-yolo-detection-export-line-format) | \<base\>.txt contains '3 0.3125 0.20833333333333334 0.3125 0.20833333333333334' (cx=(100+200/2)/640, cy=(50+100/2)/480, w=200/640, h=100/480), written at full float precision with no rounding And No file is written when there are no objects or when image width/height is 0; the class value is the real class id, not the channel index |
| YOLO Segmentation export: simplified normalized polygons | Calculation | [RULE-006](#rule-006-yolo-segmentation-export-polygon-simplification) | \<base\>_seg.txt contains '0 0.1 0.1 0.1 0.19 0.19 0.19 0.19 0.1' (outline through pixel centers; epsilon = 0.001 x perimeter) And Outlines with only 1 or 2 points are written as a closed 4-point ring (repeated point or there-and-back pair) instead of being dropped |
| Label-to-class-id resolution for YOLO, VOC and CreateML labels | Calculation | [RULE-007](#rule-007-label-text-to-class-id-resolution-on-import) | '0' -\> class 0; 'dog' -\> class 1 (alias 'dog'); 'cat' -\> class 2 (alias 'cat') |
| One exported object per segment outline (instance separation) | Calculation | [RULE-008](#rule-008-detection-and-polygon-exports-keep-same-class-objects-separate) | Two lines are written, one box per segment, instead of one merged box And Instances are computed only when YOLO Det, YOLO Seg, COCO, Pascal VOC or CreateML is selected; NPZ-only saves skip it |
| Erase subtracts pixels, splits remainders and drops tiny parts | Calculation | [RULE-009](#rule-009-eraser-splits-segments-and-drops-pieces-of-10-pixels-or-less) | Original removed; one new 'AI' mask segment of 942 px, class 2, added at the end; the 8-px part is lost |
| Class assignment for new segments and next class ID | Calculation | [RULE-011](#rule-011-new-segments-take-the-active-class-otherwise-the-next-free-class-id-highest--1) | The new segment is class 5; with class 1 active it would be class 1 |
| Class id assignment for new segments | Calculation | [RULE-011](#rule-011-new-segments-take-the-active-class-otherwise-the-next-free-class-id-highest--1) | The new segment gets class 4 And If class 3 is active the segment gets class 3; toggling the active class again deactivates it |
| Pixel priority for overlapping classes | Policy | [RULE-012](#rule-012-pixel-priority-resolves-overlapping-classes) | Ascending keeps only class 1 at that pixel; Descending keeps only class 4; non-overlapping pixels are unchanged |
| Reassign class IDs from Class Order table | Calculation | [RULE-013](#rule-013-reassign-class-ids-from-class-table-order) | 4 becomes 0, 0 becomes 1, 1 becomes 2; aliases {0: 'car'} |
| NPZ export content | Calculation | [RULE-014](#rule-014-saved-class-channel-order-is-ascending-class-id-not-the-class-order-table) | img.npz holds mask shape (480, 640, 2), class_order [0, 3], class_aliases {0: 'car', 3: 'person'} |
| NPZ one-hot mask export and class channel order | Calculation | [RULE-014](#rule-014-saved-class-channel-order-is-ascending-class-id-not-the-class-order-table) | \<base\>.npz has mask shape (480,640,2) where channel 0 is class 2 and channel 1 is class 7, class_order [2,7], and class_aliases; overlapping classes are both 1 unless pixel priority is on And The file is skipped only when the tensor has zero size; a crop that removes every pixel still writes an all-zero NPZ |
| SAM 2 propagation confidence score | Calculation | [RULE-016](#rule-016-propagation-confidence-score-per-object) | Confidence = 0.99005, which passes the default 0.99 threshold; an average of 4.59 gives 0.98995 and is flagged |
| Crop removes annotations outside the rectangle but keeps full-image coordinates | Calculation | [RULE-018](#rule-018-crop-is-clamped-to-the-image-and-blanks-everything-outside-it-on-save-including-the-last-row-and-column) | Coordinates are clamped to x2=99, y2=99, so column 99 and row 99 are zeroed; a full-image box exports with width 99/100 And Drawn crops must be wider and taller than 5 px; edges are truncated, rounded, clamped and swapped if reversed; mode switches to AI points after drawing |
| Crop zeroes saved annotations outside the crop (last row and column always lost) | Calculation | [RULE-018](#rule-018-crop-is-clamped-to-the-image-and-blanks-everything-outside-it-on-save-including-the-last-row-and-column) | Column 639 and row 479 are zeroed; the box is 639x479 px: cx = 319.5/640 = 0.49921875, cy = 239.5/480 (0.498958...), w = 639/640 = 0.9984375, h = 479/480 (0.997916...) |
| Merge selected segments into the smallest class | Calculation | [RULE-019](#rule-019-merge-selected-segments-into-the-lowest-selected-class) | Both segments become class 1 (the active class 5 is ignored) |
| Model picks the highest-scoring candidate mask | Calculation | [RULE-020](#rule-020-sam-best-mask-selection-and-click-coordinate-mapping) | Candidate 2 (score 0.94) is shown |
| Auto-convert AI masks to polygons and polygon resolution mapping | Calculation | [RULE-021](#rule-021-auto-convert-ai-masks-to-polygons) | factor = 0.000219; the polygon is built from the largest blob only and the small blob is discarded; if fewer than 3 vertices result the mask stays an AI mask |
| Archetype (suggested reference frame) selection | Calculation | [RULE-022](#rule-022-find-archetypes-suggests-about-2-of-frames-between-5-and-50-as-references) | Budget 20, minimum cluster size 10; initial allocation 12/6/1 = 19; the extra slot goes to A, so 13/6/1 frames are suggested |
| Archetype reference suggestions | Policy | [RULE-022](#rule-022-find-archetypes-suggests-about-2-of-frames-between-5-and-50-as-references) | 12 pending frames marked suggested; message 'Only 12 reference frames identified (expected ~20)' |
| Only mask-based segments seed propagation | Validation | [RULE-023](#rule-023-propagation-seeds-come-only-from-mask-segments-on-reference-frames) | Only the class-1 object is propagated (object id 1); the polygon is ignored; with no mask segments at all: 'No valid segments in reference frames' |
| Only reference frames that actually have annotations become propagation prompts | Policy | [RULE-023](#rule-023-propagation-seeds-come-only-from-mask-segments-on-reference-frames) | Objects 1 and 2 come from frame 0's in-memory masks and object 3 from frame 99's file. Frame 50 contributes nothing, is not registered with the engine, and is also excluded from receiving results, so it stays unlabeled. Object class names come from the on-screen frame's alias table, otherwise 'Class \<id\>'. |
| Propagation direction and range | Policy | [RULE-025](#rule-025-propagation-direction-and-range) | Forward pass covers frames 21-99 and backward pass covers frames 19-0; frames between the references are filled only by the forward pass And A range start of the first frame and end of the last frame are passed as 'no limit'; a skipped range start snaps to the next valid frame and a skipped end to the previous one |
| The propagation range only limits one side of each pass | Calculation | [RULE-025](#rule-025-propagation-direction-and-range) | The forward pass covers frames 11-99, including 11-49, which are outside the requested range. The backward pass is skipped because the reference (10) is not after the range start (50). With the default range 1-200, no limits are passed, so frames 11-199 (forward) and 9 down to 0 (backward) are propagated. |
| Timelines longer than the window propagate in overlapping chunks | Policy | [RULE-026](#rule-026-streaming-chunked-propagation-windows) | Chunks cover frames 0-249, 245-494 and 490-599. Frames already produced by an earlier chunk are skipped in the overlap, and reference frames are never overwritten. If the user unticks Streaming with 600 frames, a warning estimates about 7 GB (600 x 12.6 MB / 1024) and defaults to keeping Streaming on. |
| AI fragment filter | Calculation | [RULE-027](#rule-027-the-fragment-filter-keeps-only-regions-at-least-x-of-the-largest-region) | Minimum area = 0.10 x 841 = 84.1, so only the 30x30 blob is kept; the 10x10 blob is dropped although it is 11% of the largest by pixel count And If every piece is dropped or the mask has zero contour area, nothing is added ('All segments filtered out by fragment threshold'); hotkey Z toggles between 0 and the last non-zero value (100 if none was set) |
| File list checkmarks come from annotation file names next to each image | Policy | [RULE-036](#rule-036-file-list-annotation-status-indicators) | img1 shows Class Map and YOLO Detection checks (the caption counts); img2 shows a YOLO Seg check; the NPZ column is empty for both |
| COCO JSON import: categories to aliases, polygon then bbox fallback | Calculation | [RULE-037](#rule-037-coco-json-import-with-polygon-then-box-fallback) | Class 5 alias becomes 'dog.animal' and one class-5 segment covers columns 90-99, rows 90-99 (x2=min(100, 90+20)) And Multiple polygons in one annotation create one segment each; RLE (dict) segmentation falls back to bbox; missing category_id defaults to class 0; COCO aliases are merged into existing aliases |
| NPZ import: class_order mapping and legacy layouts | Calculation | [RULE-038](#rule-038-npz-import-current-and-legacy-layouts) | Two segments: class 2 from channel 0 and class 7 from channel 1; aliases become exactly {2:'cat'} And A 'masks' key is accepted as the tensor; a 'masks' (N,H,W) stack with 'class_ids' yields one segment per mask (missing id -\> 0); a 2-D mask is treated as one channel; empty channels are skipped |
| YOLO Detection import: denormalize, round half-to-even, clamp | Calculation | [RULE-040](#rule-040-yolo-detection-import-validation-rounding-and-clamping) | One segment (class resolved from label '3') fills columns 100-299 and rows 50-149 (x1=round((cx-w/2)*640)=100, x2=round((cx+w/2)*640)=300 exclusive) And Lines with other than 5 tokens or non-numeric values are silently skipped; boxes that collapse to zero width or height after clamping are dropped |
| YOLO Segmentation import: polygon rasterization | Validation | [RULE-041](#rule-041-yolo-segmentation-import-validation) | Vertices (10,10),(10,19),(19,19),(19,10) are filled into a 100-pixel class-0 segment of type 'Loaded' that also keeps the vertices And Lines with fewer than 7 tokens, an even token count, or non-numeric values are skipped; polygons that fill zero pixels are skipped |
| Propagate needs the AI extras, a reference, a SAM 2 video model and no running job | Validation | [RULE-044](#rule-044-propagation-preconditions) | Nothing starts and the user sees 'SAM 2 video predictor not available'. With a SAM 2 model loaded, propagation starts in both directions. With no model: 'SAM model not loaded'. With no reference: 'Please set a reference frame first'. While running, the button reads 'Abort' and another click cancels. |
| Edit mode requires a selected polygon or circle | Validation | [RULE-046](#rule-046-edit-mode-eligibility-and-200-vertex-limit) | The user sees 'No editable shapes selected!' and the mode stays the same. After selecting #2 and #3, R enters edit mode: #3 gets 2 handles, and #2 gets none plus the warning 'Polygon has 350 vertices (max 200 for editing)'. |
| Polygon closes when clicking near the first point | Validation | [RULE-047](#rule-047-a-polygon-needs-3-points-and-closes-when-clicking-within-the-join-distance-of-its-first-point) | The polygon is finalized (Shift held makes it an eraser); a click exactly 2.0 px away adds a fourth point instead |
| Polygon completion and join threshold | Validation | [RULE-047](#rule-047-a-polygon-needs-3-points-and-closes-when-clicking-within-the-join-distance-of-its-first-point) | (101,101), distance 1.41: polygon closes and is saved; (102,100), distance 2.0: a 4th point is added |
| Reference frames must share one image size | Validation | [RULE-048](#rule-048-reference-frames-must-match-the-first-references-image-size) | Rejected: 'Cannot add reference: image is 1280x720 but reference requires 1920x1080' |
| Reference image size lock and skipped frames | Validation | [RULE-048](#rule-048-reference-frames-must-match-the-first-references-image-size) | Adding fails with 'Cannot add reference: image is 1280x720 but reference requires 1920x1080'; during propagation frame 40 is SKIPPED and gets no mask And Clearing all references clears the size lock and the skipped marks |
| Deleting segments cannot be undone | Lifecycle | [RULE-052](#rule-052-undoredo-history-scope) | 3 segments remain; Ctrl+Z tries to undo the earlier add and reports 'Cannot undo: Segment no longer exists' |
| Undo history is unbounded, per image, and does not cover deletes or class changes | Lifecycle | [RULE-052](#rule-052-undoredo-history-scope) | The first undo targets 'add C' at index 2, finds only 2 segments, and warns 'Cannot undo: Segment no longer exists'. The second undo targets 'add B' at index 1 and removes C instead of B. |
| Undo of an erase adds a phantom segment instead of restoring | Lifecycle | [RULE-053](#rule-053-undoing-an-erase-inserts-malformed-segment-records) | (Actual) the list holds the remaining piece plus a phantom entry with no mask or vertices that receives class 3; saves add an empty class-3 channel to NPZ and a class-3 category to COCO |
| Undoing an erase does not restore the erased shapes | Lifecycle | [RULE-053](#rule-053-undoing-an-erase-inserts-malformed-segment-records) | After the erase, the polygon is gone and two class-2 'AI' mask segments exist. After undo, a third record with no shape and class 3 is added, the two pieces remain, and the original polygon is not restored. Saving writes the erased result plus an empty class-3 channel in the NPZ. Redo removes the last record again. |
| Sequence frame auto-save when changing frames | Lifecycle | [RULE-055](#rule-055-leaving-a-sequence-frame-saves-it-and-marks-it-saved-even-if-it-is-a-reference) | img_022 outputs are written; frame 22 status saved; its propagated masks cleared so it reloads from disk |
| Multi-view pair navigation always saves or deletes both images | Policy | [RULE-057](#rule-057-multi-view-batch-navigation-always-saves-ignoring-the-auto-save-setting) | p1.npz and any other p1 annotation files are deleted, p2's selected formats are written, and the next pair loads |
| Auto-save on navigate | Lifecycle | [RULE-059](#rule-059-auto-save-current-image-before-switching-images-single-view) | A is exported in all selected formats before B loads (in sequence mode the frame is marked SAVED); an A with no segments has its sidecars deleted |
| Opening another image auto-saves the current one first | Lifecycle | [RULE-059](#rule-059-auto-save-current-image-before-switching-images-single-view) | a.npz and a.txt are written. The current image becomes b.png; segments, undo history and crop reset; b.png's annotations load from b.npz; and b.png's AI embedding is computed or restored from cache immediately if a model is loaded. |
| A propagated frame is Flagged when its weakest object scores below Min Conf | Calculation | [RULE-060](#rule-060-propagated-frame-flagging-and-commit-keep-flagged-masks) | Frame 12 becomes 'flagged' with confidence 0.9850 and no masks are stored. With Keep Flagged Masks on, both masks are kept but the frame is still 'flagged' at 0.985. If the scores were 0.995 and 0.992, the frame would become 'propagated' with confidence 0.992. |
| Keep Flagged Masks decides whether partial frames keep masks | Policy | [RULE-060](#rule-060-propagated-frame-flagging-and-commit-keep-flagged-masks) | Keep Flagged OFF: no masks stored, flagged at 0.97; ON: masks A and B stored, flagged at 0.97 |
| Low-confidence frames are flagged using the minimum object score | Policy | [RULE-060](#rule-060-propagated-frame-flagging-and-commit-keep-flagged-masks) | Frame confidence 0.985, status flagged; C does not affect the score |
| AI click versus drag and minimum shape sizes | Validation | [RULE-062](#rule-062-ai-mode-click-adds-a-point-a-drag-over-5-px-draws-a-box-space-accepts-the-preview) | (104,103): positive point at the release position; (130,108): drag \>5 but box 30x8, nothing happens; (130,130): SAM box prediction And Right-click adds a negative point |
| AI mode click versus drag thresholds | Validation | [RULE-062](#rule-062-ai-mode-click-adds-a-point-a-drag-over-5-px-draws-a-box-space-accepts-the-preview) | (103,103): positive point; (130,140): box preview 'press Space to confirm'; (108,106): nothing (8x6 box too small, no point either) |
| Switching tools discards unfinished work; startup mode | Policy | [RULE-069](#rule-069-edit-mode-entry-and-mode-toggling) | The 5 points are discarded |
| Sequence initialization excludes mismatched images | Validation | [RULE-071](#rule-071-first-propagation-sets-up-the-engine-once-and-marks-wrong-size-frames-skipped) | Frame 4 skipped; message '1 frames have different dimensions (reference is 1920x1080) and will be skipped during propagation' |
| Next/previous flagged, reference and suggested navigation wraps around | Policy | [RULE-072](#rule-072-frame-status-precedence-suggestions-and-timeline-sort) | Frame 5 opens (wrap-around). From frame 3, Shift+N opens frame 90. With no flagged frames the user sees 'No more flagged frames'. |
| Timeline colors and Sort order follow review status | Policy | [RULE-072](#rule-072-frame-status-precedence-suggestions-and-timeline-sort) | The display order is 2, 3, 5, 0, 1, 4 |
| Changing Min Conf re-flags frames in the engine but not on the timeline | Policy | [RULE-073](#rule-073-min-conf-change-re-evaluates-flags-in-the-engine-but-not-on-the-timeline) | The engine no longer flags frame 20, so Save All will now save it. Frame 20 is still red on the timeline, and the flagged count and Next Flagged navigation still include it. |
| Sequence frame status lifecycle: seven states, every frame starts Pending | Lifecycle | [RULE-076](#rule-076-timeline-frame-status-lifecycle) | A 120-frame timeline is built in file-list order. Frames 0-119 are all 'pending'. References, propagated masks, confidence scores, suggestions, the skipped set and the reference size are cleared, any earlier SAM 2 engine state is discarded, and frame 0 opens. |
| Timeline trim and keep range | Lifecycle | [RULE-077](#rule-077-trim-removes-frames-from-the-timeline-only-and-re-keys-state-by-image-path) | 90 frames remain; current frame becomes old frame 20 (new index 10); no files are changed; propagation must re-initialize |
| Timeline trim and keep ranges | Validation | [RULE-077](#rule-077-trim-removes-frames-from-the-timeline-only-and-re-keys-state-by-image-path) | Cut removes frames 3-6; Keep removes frames 0-2 and 7-9; files on disk are untouched And If the current frame is removed the nearest kept frame becomes current (lower index on a tie) |
| A damaged or empty higher-priority file blocks lower-priority files | Validation | [RULE-078](#rule-078-annotations-load-from-the-best-file-present-and-a-damaged-non-npz-file-stops-the-search) | 'Error loading COCO JSON ...' is logged, 0 segments load and img.xml is never tried; navigating away with Auto-Save on then deletes both files |
| Annotation load priority chain | Policy | [RULE-078](#rule-078-annotations-load-from-the-best-file-present-and-a-damaged-non-npz-file-stops-the-search) | Only foo_coco.json is loaded; foo.txt is ignored And The chain continues to the next file only when a loader raises an exception; 'already labelled' status uses the same chain (find_annotation_file) |
| Annotation load priority chain (one format wins) | Policy | [RULE-078](#rule-078-annotations-load-from-the-best-file-present-and-a-damaged-non-npz-file-stops-the-search) | Only the 3 polygons from img_seg.txt are loaded; img.txt is ignored |
| Skip Labeled protects frames that already have annotations | Policy | [RULE-081](#rule-081-propagation-never-overwrites-reference-frames-and-by-default-frames-already-labeled) | No masks stored for frame 30; timeline shows skipped; img_030.xml untouched |
| Save All writes propagated frames except those the engine currently flags | Policy | [RULE-082](#rule-082-save-all-propagated-frames-eligibility) | Frames 11 and 12 are written in every selected format, with each object's class taken from its reference annotation (otherwise class 0, 'Class 0'), and turn 'saved' (cyan). Frame 13 is excluded, frame 14 is skipped because its masks were already cleared, the user sees 'Saved 2 frames to NPZ', and the current frame is reloaded from disk. |
| Save All writes propagated, non-flagged frames | Lifecycle | [RULE-082](#rule-082-save-all-propagated-frames-eligibility) | Frames 21, 22, 24, 25 are written from the original propagated masks; frame 23 is not saved; frame 22's manual correction is overwritten and lost |
| Saving an image with zero segments deletes all of its annotation files | Policy | [RULE-083](#rule-083-saving-an-image-with-no-segments-deletes-all-of-its-annotation-files) | img_001.npz, img_001.txt and img_001.xml are deleted (and img_001_CM.npz, img_001_seg.txt, img_001_coco.json, img_001_createml.json if present); notification 'Deleted: img_001.npz, ...'; if no file existed the warning 'No segments to save.' is shown |
| Saving an image with zero segments deletes all sidecar files | Lifecycle | [RULE-083](#rule-083-saving-an-image-with-no-segments-deletes-all-of-its-annotation-files) | foo.npz, foo_CM.npz, foo.txt, foo_seg.txt, foo_coco.json, foo.xml and foo_createml.json are removed if present, with a 'Deleted: ...' notification ('No segments to save.' if nothing existed) And Multi-view applies the same rule per viewer; sequence mode applies it on frame change |
| AI features require segment-anything and PyTorch 2.7.1 or newer | Validation | [RULE-084](#rule-084-ai-features-require-segment-anything-and-pytorch-271) | Machine A: AI available. Machine B: AI unavailable, with the log message 'PyTorch 2.7.0 found but \>=2.7.1 is required for AI features.' |
| Model discovery and type detection by file name | Policy | [RULE-085](#rule-085-model-type-detected-from-file-name) | It is treated as SAM 2; the image predictor picks the small config because '_s' appears in '_segmenter', while the video predictor checks 'large' first and picks the large config |
| Toggle Recent Class hotkey fallback | Policy | [RULE-086](#rule-086-active-class-toggle-and-recent-class-hotkey) | Class 1 becomes the active class |
| Settings file loading, fallback and legacy migration | Policy | [RULE-088](#rule-088-one-unknown-key-in-settingsjson-resets-every-preference-old-save-flags-migrate-to-export-formats) | Unknown key future_option causes all defaults: auto_save true, formats NPZ + YOLO Detection; without future_option: auto_save false and formats [NPZ] |
| Settings persistence, legacy migration and reset | Policy | [RULE-088](#rule-088-one-unknown-key-in-settingsjson-resets-every-preference-old-save-flags-migrate-to-export-formats) | Export formats become ['NPZ']; a file containing an unrecognized key instead resets all settings to defaults (NPZ + YOLO Detection, auto-save on) |
| Sequence frame load priority and per-class merging | Lifecycle | [RULE-090](#rule-090-sequence-frame-load-order-and-per-class-merge) | Frame 30 shows one class-2 segment, its YOLO Detection file has one merged box, and frame 30 becomes SAVED |
| Sequence frames load propagated masks first and merge them into one mask per class | Policy | [RULE-090](#rule-090-sequence-frame-load-order-and-per-class-merge) | The two propagated masks merge into one class-1 'Loaded' segment and frame_040.npz is ignored. When the user leaves, that single merged mask is saved, so instance formats (YOLO, COCO, VOC, CreateML) can no longer separate the two objects where they touch. |
| Linked multi-view applies accept, erase and polygon completion to both viewers | Policy | [RULE-092](#rule-092-linked-multi-view-shape-mirroring-and-class-ids) | Both polygons are added with class 2 |
| Linked multi-view operations | Policy | [RULE-092](#rule-092-linked-multi-view-shape-mirroring-and-class-ids) | Viewer 0's segment is class 2 and viewer 1's is class 1; completing polygons in both viewers instead would give both class 2 |
